<article class="agenda-item" id="item-28345">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28345">42</a> File No. DEO26-28345</h3>
<p>Resolution making certain changes in the budget of the City of Tampa for the fiscal year ending September 30, 2027; approving the transfer, reallocation, and/or appropriation of $1,066,228 within the Emergency Solutions Grants (ESG) fund for Rapid Unsheltered Survivor Housing (RUSH) Grant Activities.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<section class="agenda-item-financial" aria-labelledby="financial-28345">
<h4 id="financial-28345" class="agenda-item-section__heading">Financial impact</h4>
<p class="agenda-item-financial__statement">This resolution appropriates $1,066,228 in Emergency Solutions Grant (ESG) funds for Rapid Unsheltered Survivor Housing (RUSH) Grant activities for the Housing and Community Development Department to use to address homelessness. Funds will be appropriated within in the Emergency Solutions Grants Fund for such purposes.</p>
<dl class="agenda-item-financial__totals"><div class="agenda-item-financial__row" title="Total dollars this resolution authorizes the City to spend in the current fiscal year. Inter-fund transfers are excluded to avoid double-counting."><dt>Spending authorized</dt><dd>$1,066,228</dd></div><div class="agenda-item-financial__row" title="Outside funds (grants, fees, reimbursements) the City is committing to receive in the current fiscal year."><dt>Grant or revenue accepted</dt><dd>$1,066,228</dd></div></dl>
<h5 class="agenda-item-financial__sources-heading">Funding sources</h5>
<ul class="agenda-item-financial__sources"><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-revenue "><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$1,066,228</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">ESG Grants</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">DMG HCD ESG Rapid Unsheltered Survivor Housing (RUSH)</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Health/Hospital-ESGP Grant</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">12210.218601.331611</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Revenue Increase</span></li><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-expenditure "><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$79,967</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">ESG Grants</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">DMG HCD ESG Rapid Unsheltered Survivor Housing (RUSH)</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Staff Alloc to Oth Grants</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__project">DGM ESG Administration RUSH</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">12210.218601.512008.1002544</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Expenditure Increase</span></li><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-expenditure "><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$986,261</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">ESG Grants</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">DMG HCD ESG Rapid Unsheltered Survivor Housing (RUSH)</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Other-Contractual Services</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__project">DGM ESG Contractual RUSH</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">12210.218601.534007.1002497</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Expenditure Increase</span></li></ul>
</section>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>A special allocation of Emergency Solutions Grant (ESG) funding, called Rapid Unsheltered Survivor Housing (RUSH), in the amount of $1,000,000 was allocated to the City of Tampa during FY2025 to address the needs of homeless individuals or families or individuals or families at risk of homelessness in areas affected by Hurricanes Helene and Milton.  A second allocation in the amount t of $1,066,228 has been allocated to the City for the same purpose.</p>
</details>

<article class="agenda-item" id="item-28429">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28429">43</a> File No. DEO26-28429</h3>
<p>Resolution approving an agreement between the City of Tampa and Tampa Bay Wave Inc. providing local cash match support in an amount not to exceed $100,000.00 for the Tampa Bay Defense Tech Innovation Accelerator funded under U.S. Economic Development Administration Award no. ED26ATL0G0193.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<section class="agenda-item-financial" aria-labelledby="financial-28429">
<h4 id="financial-28429" class="agenda-item-section__heading">Financial impact</h4>
<p class="agenda-item-financial__statement">That the City of Tampa shall provide financial support in an amount $50,000 in FY2027 and $50,000 in FY2028, subject to appropriation, not to exceed a total of $100,000.00, as local cash match for eligible costs of the Tampa Bay Defense Tech Innovation Accelerator under EDA Award No. ED26ATL0G0193, subject to the terms, documentation requirements and funding availability set forth in the Agreement. Funding for such is from Development and Economic Opportunity within the General Fund.</p>
<dl class="agenda-item-financial__totals"><div class="agenda-item-financial__row" title="Total dollars this resolution authorizes the City to spend in the current fiscal year. Inter-fund transfers are excluded to avoid double-counting."><dt>Spending authorized (FY28)</dt><dd>$100,000</dd></div></dl>
<h5 class="agenda-item-financial__sources-heading">Funding sources</h5>
<ul class="agenda-item-financial__sources"><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-expenditure "><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$50,000</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">General Fund</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">Economic Opportunity Administration</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Tampa Bay Wave Contribution</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">01100.213700.582531</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Expenditure</span></li><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-expenditure "><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$50,000</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">General Fund</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">Economic Opportunity Administration</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Tampa Bay Wave Contribution</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">FY28 · 01100.213700.582531</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Expenditure</span></li></ul>
</section>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>Tampa Bay Wave, Inc. was awarded federal financial assistance from the U.S. Department of Commerce, Economic Development Administration under Award No. ED26ATL0G0193 for the Tampa Bay Defense Tech Innovation Accelerator.  The award included a federal share of $1,377,318.00 and a recipient match of $342,000.00, for a total project cost of $1,719,318.00.</p>
<p>The City previously committed $100,000.00 in local cash support toward the required recipient match. Under the proposed agreement, the City will provide funding in an amount not to exceed $100,000.00 with $50,000.00 provided in Fiscal Year 2027 and $50,000.00 provided in Fiscal Year 2028.</p>
<p>The Tampa Bay Defense Tech Innovation Accelerator is designed to strengthen regional economic resiliency through defense technology innovation and will support two cohorts of fifteen startups each.  Program activities include accelerator programming, technical assistance, mentoring, pitch coaching, strategic introductions, access to capital, workforce development and post-cohort support.</p>
</details>
<div class="agenda-item__docs">
<p class="agenda-item__label">Supporting documents</p>
<ul>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-19/meeting-2941/28429/summary-sheet-cover-sheet.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Summary Sheet- COVER SHEET</a></li>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-19/meeting-2941/28429/reso-amp-agreement-tampa-bay-wave-eda-grant-matching-funds.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">RESO &amp;AMP; AGREEMENT - TAMPA BAY WAVE EDA GRANT MATCHING FUNDS.PDF</a></li>
</ul>
</div>
</article>
<article class="agenda-item" id="item-28468">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28468">44</a> File No. DEO26-28468</h3>
<p>Resolution approving the acceptance of a Warranty Deed from Nevine Robidoux, Successor Trustee of the GJ Morter Revocable Trust dated July 31, 2024, to the City of Tampa for the conveyance of real property located at 1918 East Hemlock Avenue by donation for wastewater purposes, the approximate value is Thirty-Five Thousand Five Hundred Fifty-Five Dollars ($35,555), together with closing costs of Two Thousand Dollars ($2,000).</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<section class="agenda-item-financial" aria-labelledby="financial-28468">
<h4 id="financial-28468" class="agenda-item-section__heading">Financial impact</h4>
<p class="agenda-item-financial__statement">This resolution provides funding in the amount of $2,000.00 for the title fees and closing costs associated with the donation of real property located at 1918 Hemlock Avenue for the Wastewater Department within the Wastewater Operations Fund.</p>
<dl class="agenda-item-financial__totals"><div class="agenda-item-financial__row" title="Total dollars this resolution authorizes the City to spend in the current fiscal year. Inter-fund transfers are excluded to avoid double-counting."><dt>Spending authorized (FY27)</dt><dd>$2,000</dd></div></dl>
<h5 class="agenda-item-financial__sources-heading">Funding sources</h5>
<ul class="agenda-item-financial__sources"><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-expenditure "><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$2,000</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">Wastewater - Operations</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">Wastewater Engineering Administration</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Other-Professional Services</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">FY27 · 41000.243500.531002</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Expenditure</span></li></ul>
</section>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>Nevine Robidoux, Successor Trustee of the GJ Morter Revocable Trust dated July 31, 2024 desires to convey by donation to the City of Tampa, real property located at 1918 East Hemlock Avenue for wastewater purposes.  The City is willing to accept the Warranty Deed as it is in the City&#39;s best interest to accept the donation. The City will be paying $2,000 for the title fees and closing costs associated with this donation of real property located at 1918 Hemlock Avenue.</p>
</details>

Darville for the construction and operation of certain dock structures and facilities over or on a portion of submerged sovereign lands owned and held by the City in the vicinity of 4315 North River View Avenue, Tampa, Florida.</p>

<p>Resolution approving the acceptance of a Grant of Sidewalk Easement from Interbay Commons, LLC, to the City of Tampa for the purpose of installing, constructing, maintaining, operating, repairing and/or replacing sidewalk improvements at 6301 and 6302 Interbay Commons Way, Tampa, Florida.</p>

Interbay Commons, LLC has agreed to convey the sidewalk easement to the City.  It is in the City&#39;s best interests to accept the Grant of Sidewalk Easement for said purposes.</p>

<article class="agenda-item" id="item-28473">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28473">49</a> File No. DEO26-28473</h3>
<p>Resolution approving the execution by the Mayor and attestation by the City Clerk of a Private Dock Agreement between the City of Tampa and Erol and Kathryn Belli for the construction and operation of certain dock structures and facilities over or on a portion of submerged sovereign lands owned and held by the City of Tampa in the vicinity of 2203 North Riverside Drive, Tampa, Florida.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>The City owns certain submerged sovereign lands below the Hillsborough River that abut real property at 2203 North Riverside Drive. Erol and Kathryn Belli own the upland property and enjoy certain riparian rights to the waters of the Hillsborough River. The Belli&#39;s have obtained Minor Work Permit Number 78274(R1) from the Environmental Protection Commission for the construction of certain dock structures.  A non-covered boatlift and existing riprap are included as part of the dock facilities.  The dock facilities will be located on a portion of City owned land.  The parties desire to enter into the Private Dock Agreement.</p>
</details>
<div class="agenda-item__docs">

<article class="agenda-item" id="item-28477">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28477">50</a> File No. DEO26-28477</h3>
<p>Resolution approving the execution by the Mayor and attestation by the City Clerk of a Termination of Right-of-Way Encroachment Agreement between the City of Tampa and RD RWD Tampa, LLC as it pertains to a right-of-way encroachment between city and Hillsborough River Realty Corp. dated September 16, 2013.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>This agreement formally terminates the 2013 Right-of-Way Encroachment Agreement authorizing the construction of a pedestrian skybridge over the City’s Parker Street right-of-way. Originally executed with Hillsborough River Realty Corp., the agreement was assigned to RD RWD Tampa, LLC in 2022 following the transfer of property ownership. Because the skybridge was never constructed and the current owner no longer intends to pursue it, the parties have agreed to terminate the agreement, eliminate the authorization for the proposed skybridge, and release each other from any remaining rights and obligations.</p>
</details>

<p class="agenda-item__label">Supporting documents</p>

<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-19/meeting-2941/28477/parker-street-termination-of-right-of-way-encroachment-agreement-v2.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">PARKER STREET-TERMINATION OF RIGHT-OF-WAY ENCROACHMENT AGREEMENT V2.PDF</a></li>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-19/meeting-2941/28477/parker-street-recorded-assignment-and-assumption.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">PARKER STREET-RECORDED ASSIGNMENT AND ASSUMPTION.PDF</a></li>

</ul>
</div>
</article>
<article class="agenda-item" id="item-28698">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28698">51</a> File No. DEO26-28698</h3>
<p>Resolution approving the execution by the Mayor and attestation by the City Clerk of a Grant of Utility Easement in favor of Tampa Electric Company for the provision of electrical service to City-owned property located at 3420 East Clark Street, Tampa, Florida.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>Tampa Electric Company has requested an easement from the City for the provision of electrical service over, across, under and through City-owned property at 3420 E Clark St.</p>
<p>The City is willing to grant this easement. It is in the best interest of the City to approve the execution of the Grant of Utility Easement.</p>
</details>

<h3 class="agenda__committee" id="mobility-and-asset-management-committee">Mobility and Asset Management Committee</h3>
<article class="agenda-item" id="item-27869">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-27869">52</a> File No. MAM26-27869</h3>
<p>Resolution approving the renewal of an award between the City of Tampa and Merion Landscape Services LLC, with a 4.2% Consumer Price Index (CPI) price increase, for the furnishing of Ditch Mowing and Maintenance Services for North Zones (Sheltered Market Solicitation) in the estimated amount of $355,000 for use by the Mobility Department.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<section class="agenda-item-financial" aria-labelledby="financial-27869">
<h4 id="financial-27869" class="agenda-item-section__heading">Financial impact</h4>
<p class="agenda-item-financial__statement">This Resolution renews the award between the City and Merion Landscape Services LLC for the furnishing of Ditch Mowing and Maintenance Services for North Zones (Sheltered Market Solicitation) in the estimated amount of $355,000, over a 12-month term, subject to annual appropriation, for use by the Mobility Department within the Stormwater Service Assessment Fund.</p>
<dl class="agenda-item-financial__totals"><div class="agenda-item-financial__row" title="Total dollars this resolution authorizes the City to spend in the current fiscal year. Inter-fund transfers are excluded to avoid double-counting."><dt>Spending authorized (FY27)</dt><dd>$320,000</dd></div></dl>
<p class="agenda-item-financial__future">Plus <strong>$35,000</strong> in future-year amounts subject to annual Council approval.</p>
<h5 class="agenda-item-financial__sources-heading">Funding sources</h5>
<ul class="agenda-item-financial__sources"><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-expenditure "><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$320,000</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">Stormwater Service Assessment Fund</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">TSS - Stormwater Operations</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Repair and Maintenance Services</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">FY27 · 10700.234300.546000</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Estimated Expenditure</span></li><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-expenditure is-future"><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$35,000</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">Stormwater Service Assessment Fund</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">TSS - Stormwater Operations</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Repair and Maintenance Services</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">FY28 · 10700.234300.546000</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Estimated Expenditure</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__subject" title="Council is not committing this fiscal year today; subject to future-year appropriation.">subject to annual appropriation</span></li></ul>
</section>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>It is recommended that the City renew its existing award with Merion Landscape Services LLC for the provision of Ditch Mowing and Maintenance Services for North Zones (Sheltered Market Solicitation) for use by the Mobility Department for an additional one year term.  The initial award was made in November 2024.  This award is in its first renewal term.</p>
<p>Merion Landscape Services LLC provides satisfactory service, quality of product and performance. The Mobility Department has reviewed the prices and feels that it is in the best interest of the City to renew the award for an additional one-year period. Merion Landscape Services LLC has agreed to renew Ditch Mowing and Maintenance Services for North Zones (Sheltered Market Solicitation) with a Consumer Price Index (CPI) price escalation of 4.2% which is allowed per the bid.</p>
<p>Equal Business Opportunity (EBO) Statement: Pursuant to the review by the Equal Business Opportunity (EBO) Division, this one-year renewal contract is in compliance.  Merion Landscape Services LLC is a City Certified Small Local Business Enterprise (SLBE).</p>
</details>

<p class="agenda-item__label">Supporting documents</p>
<ul>

<p>It is recommended that the City renew its existing award with Merion Landscape Services LLC for the provision of Ditch Mowing and Maintenance Services for Central and South Zones for use by the Mobility Department for an additional one-year term. This award is in its first renewal term which was extended to December 6, 2026, per the bid document Section 4.13.1 Supplemental Unilateral Renewal Periods.</p>

Merion Landscape Services LLC has agreed to renew Ditch Mowing and Maintenance Services for Central and South Zones with a Consumer Price Index (CPI) price escalation of 4.2% which is allowed per the bid.</p>

<article class="agenda-item" id="item-28110">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28110">54</a> File No. MAM26-28110</h3>
<p>Resolution approving an Agreement between the City of Tampa and Ennead LLC for the provision of Stormwater Utility Data Management-Assessment Roll Production in the estimated amount of $49,500 for use by the Mobility Department, Stormwater Division.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<section class="agenda-item-financial" aria-labelledby="financial-28110">
<h4 id="financial-28110" class="agenda-item-section__heading">Financial impact</h4>
<p class="agenda-item-financial__statement">This Resolution approves an Agreement between the City of Tampa and Ennead LLC for the provision of Stormwater Utility Data Management-Assessment Roll Production and provides an estimated amount of $49,500, over a 12-month term, for use by the Mobility Department, Stormwater Division within the Stormwater Service Assessment Fund and the Stormwater Improvement Assessment Fund.</p>
<dl class="agenda-item-financial__totals"><div class="agenda-item-financial__row" title="Total dollars this resolution authorizes the City to spend in the current fiscal year. Inter-fund transfers are excluded to avoid double-counting."><dt>Spending authorized (FY27)</dt><dd>$49,500</dd></div></dl>
<h5 class="agenda-item-financial__sources-heading">Funding sources</h5>
<ul class="agenda-item-financial__sources"><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-expenditure "><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$24,750</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">Stormwater Service Assessment Fund</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">TSS - Stormwater Engineering</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Other-Contractual Services</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">FY27 · 10700.242000.534007</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Estimated Expenditure</span></li><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-expenditure "><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$24,750</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">Stormwater Improv Assessment Cap Fund</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">Stormwater Capital Project Costs</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Other-Professional Services</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">FY27 · 31700.241800.531002</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Estimated Expenditure</span></li></ul>
</section>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<ol><li>Department or Location of Project: Mobility Department, Stormwater Division</li><li>Total number of vendors that accessed and downloaded the proposal: 39</li><li>Number and List of proposers: three, Ennead, LLC, Raftelis Financial Consultants, Inc., and Anagha Solutions Inc</li><li>Agreement amount: $49,500.00</li><li>Why the winning proposal was selected: The evaluation committee selected the most responsible and responsive Proposer whose proposal meets the needs of the City to the best degree.</li></ol><p>Equal Business Opportunity (EBO) Division Statement:  Ennead, LLC is a City of Tampa Certified Firm.</p>
<p>It is recommended that the City award Stormwater Utility Data Management-Assessment Roll Production to Ennead, LLC for use by the Mobility Department. The term of the agreement is for a (1) one-year term with (4) four additional (1) one-year renewal options. The Department has reviewed and approved the proposal response from Ennead, LLC and comments were supplied via Department recommendation letter.</p>
<p>This award was made through the Request for Proposal process. Proposers are eligible to earn up to 15 points (out of a possible 100) for EBO participation.</p>
</details>

<article class="agenda-item" id="item-28522">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28522">55</a> File No. MAM26-28522</h3>
<p>Resolution authorizing an Agreement for Consultant Services in the amount of $900,000 between the City of Tampa and Mead and Hunt, Inc. in connection with Contract 26-D-00011 Smart Mobility General Engineering Contract.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<section class="agenda-item-financial" aria-label="Financial impact">
<h4 class="agenda-item-section__heading">Financial impact</h4>
<p class="agenda-item-financial__statement">This Resolution approves an agreement between the City of Tampa and Mead and Hunt, Inc. in the amount of $900,000, controlled by requisition, within the Local Option Gas Tax Capital Projects Fund for use by the Mobility Department.</p>
<ul class="agenda-item-financial__sources"><li class="agenda-item-financial__source">$900,000 FY27 (Estimated Expenditures), controlled by requisition</li></ul>
</section>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<ol><li>Background: a. Contract Title: Smart Mobility General Engineering Contract b. Address of Project: Various locations throughout the City c. Number of bidders/submitters: Five (5) d.    Names of bidders/submitters: •EJB Engineering, LLC • HBC Engineering Company • Iteris, Inc •Kimley-Horn and Associates, Inc.</li><li>Dollar amount: $900,000.00</li><li>Why the awardee was selected: Selected Pursuant to FS 287.055 CCNA.</li><li>Contractor/Firm: Mead and Hunt, Inc.</li><li>Business Case:  This Agreement provides for a wide range of professional engineering services to assist the Mobility Department with the Smart Mobility program. Project  will support innovative techniques and best practices that will maximize the value and effectiveness of this technology, enhance operational performance, and improve overall system outcomes.</li><li>Equal Business Opportunity (EBO) Statement: Mead and Hunt, Inc., the prime contractor, is committed to utilizing two (2) city-certified firms for subcontracting.</li><li>Sustainability and Resiliency: This project is consistent with Transforming Tampa&#39;s Tomorrow goal of Sustainability and Resiliency and is integral to the Smart Mobility program.</li><li>Project: a.    Contract delivery method: This is a Design-Bid- Build project.</li></ol><p>•Mead and Hunt, Inc.</p>
<p>Services will include, but not limited to: advancing Safety, Mobility, Automated, Real-time Traffic Management (SMART) Corridor monitoring, assisting in identifying data sources for best practices in congestion management, assessing Intelligent Transportation Systems (ITS) and detection device features with optimizing applications, developing prioritization tools for traffic signal installation, upgrades, and removals, identifying passive detection locations for the bicycle network, and other technical support as needed.</p>
<p>This is a three (3) year contract, project will be managed through a series of subsequently issued task orders.</p>
<p>Community Engagement: The Mobility Department staff will assist the Firm in the communication efforts to ensure transparency and integration of the stakeholders.</p>
<p>b. Placemaking: N/A c. Time, Quality Assurance, Cost Monitoring (Budget) •  365 consecutive calendar days.</p>
<p>• The e-Builder project management software system will be utilized to optimize the monitoring of schedules and costs. This software was recently purchased by the City of Tampa and is a premier system for construction project management.</p>
</details>
<div class="agenda-item__docs">
<p class="agenda-item__label">Supporting documents</p>
<ul>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-19/meeting-2941/28522/summary-sheet-cover-sheet.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Summary Sheet- COVER SHEET</a></li>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-19/meeting-2941/28522/r26-d-00011consultsvcagmt-r1.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">R26-D-00011CONSULTSVCAGMT-R1.PDF</a></li>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-19/meeting-2941/28522/26-d-00011-rfqcrevu.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">26-D-00011 RFQCREVU.PDF</a></li>
</ul>
</div>
</article>
</section>

<section class="agenda__section">
<h2 id="items-being-set-for-public-hearings-by-council-consent">Items Being Set for Public Hearings by Council Consent</h2>
<article class="agenda-item" id="item-28523">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28523">56</a> File No. PH26-28523</h3>
<p>Resolution calling for a public hearing on December 17, 2026, at 10:30 a.m., or as soon thereafter, in City Council Chambers, 315 E. Kennedy Blvd., 3rd Floor, Tampa, FL 33602, pursuant to section 180.191, Florida Statutes on proposed changes to the schedule of wastewater disposal rates and monthly base charges for all City of Tampa wastewater customers within the City of Tampa service area; providing for notice.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>The City of Tampa has the authority to construct and operate a sewer utility.  Section 180.191, Florida Statutes, requires a public hearing before charging rates, fees, and charges.</p>
<p>The City of Tampa is proposing amended wastewater rates in order to comply with section 180.191, Florida Statutes.  This resolution is scheduling a public hearing to allow the public and all users of the City’s wastewater system, owners, tenants, or occupants of property served or to be served thereby, and all others interested an opportunity to be heard.</p>
</details>
<div class="agenda-item__docs">
<p class="agenda-item__label">Supporting documents</p>
<ul>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-19/meeting-2941/28523/summary-sheet-cover-sheet.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Summary Sheet- COVER SHEET</a></li>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-19/meeting-2941/28523/wastewater-rate-ph-resolution-.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">WASTEWATER RATE PH RESOLUTION .PDF</a></li>
</ul>
</div>
</article>
<article class="agenda-item" id="item-28525">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28525">57</a> File No. PH26-28525</h3>
<p>Resolution calling for a public hearing on December 17, 2026, at 10:30 a.m., or as soon thereafter, in City Council Chambers, 315 E. Kennedy Blvd., 3rd Floor, Tampa, FL 33602, pursuant to section 180.191, Florida Statutes on proposed changes to the schedule of water usage rates and monthly base charges for all City of Tampa water customers within the City of Tampa service area; providing for notice.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>Pursuant to Chapter 180, Florida Statutes, the City of Tampa has the authority to construct and operate a water utility.  Section 180.191, Florida Statutes, requires a public hearing before charging rates, fees, and charges. This resolution is scheduling a public hearing to allow the public and the all users of the City of Tampa’s water system, owners, tenants, or occupants of property served or to be served thereby, and all others interested an opportunity to be heard.</p>
</details>
<div class="agenda-item__docs">

</ul>
</div>
</article>
<article class="agenda-item" id="item-28526">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28526">58</a> File No. PH26-28526</h3>
<p>Resolution calling for a public hearing on December 17, 2026, at 10:30 a.m., or as soon thereafter, in City Council Chambers, 315 E. Kennedy Blvd., 3rd Floor, Tampa, FL 33602, pursuant to section 180.191, Florida Statutes on proposed changes to the schedule of water connection fees for all City of Tampa water customers within the City of Tampa service area; providing for notice.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>The City of Tampa has the authority to construct and operate a water utility.  Section 180.191, Florida Statutes, requires a public hearing before charging rates, fees, and charges.  This resolution is scheduling a public hearing to allow the public and all users of the City of Tampa’s water system, owners, tenants, or occupants of property served or to be served thereby, and all others interested an opportunity to be heard.</p>
</details>
<div class="agenda-item__docs">
<p class="agenda-item__label">Supporting documents</p>
<ul>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-19/meeting-2941/28526/summary-sheet-cover-sheet.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Summary Sheet- COVER SHEET</a></li>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-19/meeting-2941/28526/water-connection-rates-ph-reso.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">WATER CONNECTION RATES - PH RESO.PDF</a></li>
</ul>
</div>
</article>
<article class="agenda-item" id="item-28475">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28475">59</a> File No. BG26-28475</h3>
<p>Resolution setting a public hearing for December 10, 2026 at 5:01 P.M., or as soon thereafter, in City Council Chambers, 315 E. Kennedy Blvd., 3rd Floor, Tampa, FL 33602, before Tampa City Council to consider the draft Consolidated Annual Performance and Evaluation Report to The U.S. Department of Housing and Urban Development regarding the City’s Community Development Block Grant, Emergency Solutions Grant, Home Investment Partnerships, and Housing Opportunities For Persons With Aids Programs for the 2025 Program and 2026 Fiscal Year that ended on September 30, 2026.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>The City of Tampa is the recipient of federal funds under Title I of the Housing and Community Development Act of 1974 (PL 93-383) from the U.S. Department of Housing and Urban Development (“HUD”) for the City’s Community Development Block Grant, Emergency Solutions Grant, HOME Investment Partnerships and Housing Opportunities for Persons with AIDS Programs. The City is required by federal law and regulations to provide a Consolidated Annual Performance and Evaluation Report (“CAPER”) to HUD summarizing the activities undertaken and the progress achieved in the Programs during the 2025 Program/2026 Fiscal Year that ended on September 30, 2026.</p>
<p>The City’s Citizen Participation Plan for these Programs requires that a public hearing be conducted by Tampa City Council regarding the CAPER before it is submitted to HUD; to provide fifteen (15) days’ advance public notice of and availability of the draft Annual Performance Report or CAPER before the public hearing; and make a complete copy of the draft Annual Performance Report or CAPER available for public review and comment for a fifteen (15) day comment period.</p>
<p>The CAPER is due to HUD on or before December 28, 2026.</p>
</details>
<div class="agenda-item__docs">
<p class="agenda-item__label">Supporting documents</p>
<ul>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-19/meeting-2941/28475/summary-sheet-cover-sheet.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Summary Sheet- COVER SHEET</a></li>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-19/meeting-2941/28475/reso-setting-public-hearing-caper-py2025.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">RESO SETTING PUBLIC HEARING CAPER PY2025.PDF</a></li>
</ul>
</div>
</article>
</section>

<p>FY27-FY31 (UNAN, Clendenin absent at vote, Young absent) - An Ordinance amending the Tampa Comprehensive Plan, Capital Improvement Program and Capital Improvements Section schedule of projects by replacing the fiscal year 2026 through fiscal year 2030 Capital Improvements Section schedule of projects with the fiscal year 2027 through fiscal year 2031 Capital Improvements Section schedule of projects; providing repeal of all ordinances in conflict.</p>

<article class="agenda-item" id="item-28838">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28838">64</a> File No. E2026-8</h3>
<p>CH 2 (UNAN) - An ordinance relating to the government of the City of Tampa, Florida, submitting to the electors of the City a proposed amendment to the Revised Charter of the City of Tampa of 1975, as amended, to create Section 2.15; providing the ability to City Council to override and thus render void an executive order promulgated by the Mayor under the charter.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>It creates Section 2.15 of the Tampa Charter giving City Council the authority, by an affirmative vote of at least five members acting by resolution, to override and void a mayoral executive order issued under the Charter, and it bars the Mayor from reissuing that order without Council approval.</p>
</details>
<div class="agenda-item__docs">
<p class="agenda-item__label">Supporting documents</p>
<ul>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-19/meeting-2941/28838/summary-sheet-cover-sheet.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Summary Sheet- COVER SHEET</a></li>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-19/meeting-2941/28838/proposed-charter-amendment-change-final-ordinance-for-section-2.15.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">PROPOSED CHARTER AMENDMENT CHANGE - FINAL ORDINANCE FOR SECTION 2.15.PDF</a></li>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-19/meeting-2941/28838/bie-proposed-charter-amendment-change-final-ordinance-for-section-2.15.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">BIE - PROPOSED CHARTER AMENDMENT CHANGE - FINAL ORDINANCE FOR SECTION 2.15.PDF</a></li>
</ul>
</div>
</article>
<article class="agenda-item" id="item-28839">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28839">65</a> File No. E2026-8</h3>
<p>CH 2 (UNAN) - An ordinance relating to the government of the City of Tampa, Florida, submitting to the electors of the city a proposed amendment to the Revised Charter of the City of Tampa of 1975, as amended, to amend Section 2.04 to clarify that City Council may create and fill additional staff positions to assist City Council as long as the funds are allocated within City Council’s budget; removing the requirement for prior mayoral approval.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>This amendment to Section 2.04 of the Tampa Charter would clarify that City Council may create and fill additional staff positions for any Council function and remove the requirement for prior mayoral approval, provided funding is available within the Council’s budget.</p>
</details>
<div class="agenda-item__docs">
<p class="agenda-item__label">Supporting documents</p>

<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-19/meeting-2941/28839/substitute-proposed-charter-amendment-change-final-ordinance-for-section-2.04.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">SUBSTITUTE - PROPOSED CHARTER AMENDMENT CHANGE - FINAL ORDINANCE FOR SECTION 2.04.PDF</a></li>
</ul>
</div>
</article>
<article class="agenda-item" id="item-28840">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28840">66</a> File No. E2026-8</h3>
<p>CH 2 (UNAN) - An Ordinance relating to the government of the City of Tampa, Florida; submitting to the electors of the City a proposed amendment to the revised Charter of the City of Tampa of 1975, as amended, to amend Section 2.03 clarifying City Council’s ability to require their member’s attendance.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>This amendment to Section 2.03 of the Tampa Charter would clarify City Council’s authority to adopt its own bylaws, regulations, and procedural rules, and would change the requirement for member attendance at meetings from “compel” to “require.”</p>
</details>
<div class="agenda-item__docs">
<p class="agenda-item__label">Supporting documents</p>

<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-19/meeting-2941/28840/proposed-charter-amendment-change-final-ordinance-for-section-2.03.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">PROPOSED CHARTER AMENDMENT CHANGE - FINAL ORDINANCE FOR SECTION 2.03.PDF</a></li>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-19/meeting-2941/28840/bie-proposed-charter-amendment-change-final-ordinance-for-section-2.03.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">BIE - PROPOSED CHARTER AMENDMENT CHANGE - FINAL ORDINANCE FOR SECTION 2.03.PDF</a></li>

</div>
</article>
<article class="agenda-item" id="item-28841">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28841">67</a> File No. E2026-8</h3>
<p>CH 2 (UNAN) - An ordinance relating to the government of the City of Tampa, Florida; submitting to the electors of the city a proposed amendment to the Revised Charter of the City of Tampa of 1975, as amended, to amend Section 2.08 clarifying that the ordinance reading procedures must follow general law and clarifying that three City Council members must vote in the affirmative to require the whole text of a proposed ordinance be read at final reading, as opposed to only the title of the ordinance, when general law requires two readings of the ordinance.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>It clarifies in Section 2.08 of the Tampa Charter that general law governs ordinance-reading procedures and that three City Council members must vote to require a full reading of the ordinance text on final reading instead of only the title.</p>
</details>
<div class="agenda-item__docs">
<p class="agenda-item__label">Supporting documents</p>

<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-19/meeting-2941/28841/summary-sheet-cover-sheet.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Summary Sheet- COVER SHEET</a></li>

<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-19/meeting-2941/28841/substitute-proposed-charter-amendment-change-final-ordinance-for-section-2.08.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">SUBSTITUTE - PROPOSED CHARTER AMENDMENT CHANGE - FINAL ORDINANCE FOR SECTION 2.08.PDF</a></li>
</ul>
</div>
</article>
<article class="agenda-item" id="item-28842">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28842">68</a> File No. E2026-8</h3>
<p>CH 4 (Miranda &amp; Carlson no) - An Ordinance relating to the government of the City of Tampa, Florida; submitting to the electors of the City a proposed amendment to the Revised Charter of the City of Tampa of 1975, as amended, to amend Section 4.01 and create Section 4.03 to strike the current mayoral term limit provision and replace it with a more restrictive provision that mandates no person who has served as mayor for more than six years may be elected as mayor again; providing that such provision applies only to time served as mayor after adoption of said amendment.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>This amendment would revise Section 4.01 and add Section 4.03 of the Tampa Charter to replace the current mayoral term-limit provision with a stricter rule. Under the change, anyone who has served more than six years as mayor after the amendment takes effect would no longer be eligible to be elected as mayor again.</p>
</details>
<div class="agenda-item__docs">
<p class="agenda-item__label">Supporting documents</p>
<ul>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-19/meeting-2941/28842/summary-sheet-cover-sheet.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Summary Sheet- COVER SHEET</a></li>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-19/meeting-2941/28842/proposed-charter-amendment-change-final-ordinance-for-sections-4.01-and-4.03.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">PROPOSED CHARTER AMENDMENT CHANGE - FINAL ORDINANCE FOR SECTIONS 4.01 AND 4.03.PDF</a></li>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-19/meeting-2941/28842/bie-proposed-charter-amendment-change-final-ordinance-for-sections-4.01-and-4.03.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">BIE - PROPOSED CHARTER AMENDMENT CHANGE - FINAL ORDINANCE FOR SECTIONS 4.01 AND 4.03.PDF</a></li>
</ul>
</div>
</article>
<article class="agenda-item" id="item-28843">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28843">69</a> File No. E2026-8</h3>
<p>CH 6 (UNAN) - An ordinance relating to the government of the City of Tampa, Florida; submitting to the electors of the City a proposed amendment to the Revised Charter of the City of Tampa of 1975, as amended, to amend Section 6.02 clarifying the four-year term of office under this section applies only to the mayor and City Council members and removing language referencing 1979 adoption.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>It clarifies that Section 6.02’s four-year term applies only to the mayor and City Council members and removes outdated language referencing the 1979 municipal election.</p>
</details>
<div class="agenda-item__docs">
<p class="agenda-item__label">Supporting documents</p>
<ul>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-19/meeting-2941/28843/summary-sheet-cover-sheet.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Summary Sheet- COVER SHEET</a></li>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-19/meeting-2941/28843/proposed-charter-amendment-change-final-ordinance-for-section-6.02.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">PROPOSED CHARTER AMENDMENT CHANGE - FINAL ORDINANCE FOR SECTION 6.02.PDF</a></li>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-19/meeting-2941/28843/bie-proposed-charter-amendment-change-final-ordinance-for-section-6.02.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">BIE - PROPOSED CHARTER AMENDMENT CHANGE - FINAL ORDINANCE FOR SECTION 6.02.PDF</a></li>
</ul>
</div>
</article>
<article class="agenda-item" id="item-28844">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28844">70</a> File No. E2026-8</h3>
<p>CH 6 (UNAN) - An ordinance relating to the government of the City of Tampa, Florida; submitting to the electors of the City a proposed amendment to the Revised Charter of the City of Tampa of 1975, as amended, to amend Section 6.06 to remove obsolete language; clarify that a majority vote under said section requires no fewer than four members of City Council to increase or decrease salaries of officers of the City; provide that City Council may act under said section pursuant to resolution or ordinance.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>It clarifies that Section 6.06 requires at least four City Council members to vote in favor of any change to city officers’ salaries and specifies that Council may take such action through either a resolution or an ordinance.</p>
</details>
<div class="agenda-item__docs">
<p class="agenda-item__label">Supporting documents</p>
<ul>
<li><a href="https://docs.meetings.tampamonitor.com/2026-11-19/meeting-2941/28844/summary-sheet-cover-sheet.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Summary Sheet- COVER SHEET</a></li>

<article class="agenda-item" id="item-28845">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28845">71</a> File No. E2026-8</h3>
<p>CH 6 (UNAN) - An ordinance relating to the government of the City of Tampa, Florida; submitting to the electors of the city a proposed amendment to the Revised Charter of the City of Tampa of 1975, as amended, to amend Section 6.05 to remove the obsolete residency requirement reference in the oath of office for non-elected City officers.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>It submits to voters a Charter amendment to Section 6.05 removing the obsolete residency-requirement reference from the oath of office for non-elected city officers, eliminating outdated language tied to a former residency rule.</p>
</details>

<p>CH 7 (UNAN) - An ordinance relating to the government of the City of Tampa, Florida; submitting to the electors of the city a proposed amendment to the Revised Charter of the City of Tampa of 1975, as amended, to amend Section 7.05 to ensure accordance with state law.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>

<article class="agenda-item" id="item-28847">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28847">73</a> File No. E2026-8</h3>
<p>CH 7 (UNAN) - An ordinance relating to the government of the City of Tampa, Florida; submitting to the electors of the City a proposed amendment to the Revised Charter of the City of Tampa of 1975, as amended, to amend Section 7.07 to allow City Council to initiate midyear budget changes without the need for mayoral recommendation and in a manner consistent with general law.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>If approved, this amendment to section 7.07 of the Tampa Charter would remove the requirement for a Mayor’s recommendation before City Council reallocates unused budgeted funds or appropriates unappropriated cash surplus for any lawful municipal purpose. .</p>
</details>

<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28848">74</a> File No. E2026-8</h3>
<p>CH 9 (UNAN) - An ordinance relating to the government of the City of Tampa, Florida; submitting to the electors of the city a proposed amendment to the Revised Charter of the City of Tampa of 1975, as amended, to amend Section 9.02 to remove the obsolete requirement that the chief building inspector shall be the ex officio director of the Zoning Adjustment Board.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>It amends Section 9.02 of the Tampa Charter to remove the obsolete provision stating that the chief building official serves as an ex officio director on the zoning adjustment board, eliminating outdated language.</p>

<article class="agenda-item" id="item-28849">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28849">75</a> File No. E2026-8</h3>
<p>CH 10 (UNAN) - An ordinance relating to the government of the City of Tampa, Florida; submitting to the electors of the City a proposed amendment to the Revised Charter of the City of Tampa of 1975, as amended, to amend Section 10.07 to provide a procedure for citizen’s initiatives and referendums that is in accordance with general law.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>This measure would amend Section 10.07 of the Tampa Charter to set citizen-initiated and referendum procedures by ordinance consistent with state law, allow changes to those procedures only every two years and only 90 days after a general election, and prohibit initiatives or referendums on zoning and comprehensive plan amendments as required by statute.</p>
</details>

<article class="agenda-item" id="item-28852">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28852">78</a> File No. E2026-8</h3>
<p>CH 12 (UNAN) - An ordinance relating to the government of the City of Tampa, Florida; submitting to the electors of the City a proposed amendment to the Revised Charter of the City of Tampa of 1975, as amended, to create Article XII, Section 12.01 creating the appointed position of and an Office of Inspector General; setting the office’s purpose; providing for independent activity and non-interference by City officials; authorizing use of legal and human resources city services; requiring an implementation ordinance be adopted on a certain schedule; specifying the role of the office under the charter; granting certain powers including subpoena authority; requiring cooperation by city officers, employees, and contractors; setting appointment method, term, removal method, and process to fill vacancies by City Council; providing for office budget request process.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>would add Article XII, Section 12.01 to the Tampa Charter to establish an Inspector General who operates independently from the City’s executive and legislative branches, empowered to conduct investigations, audits, and reviews. The amendment requires the City Council to adopt an implementing ordinance defining the office’s authority and procedures, and it places the Inspector General’s appointment, term, and removal under City Council oversight.</p>
</details>

<section class="agenda__section">
<h2 id="1-30-p-m-petitions-to-vacate-and-public-hearings-on-legislative-matters">1:30 P.M. - Petitions to Vacate and Public Hearings on Legislative Matters</h2>
<article class="agenda-item" id="item-28315">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28315">81</a> File No. PH26-27129</h3>
<p>Public hearing to consider whether to opt-out of the property tax exemption for certain multifamily projects that provide affordable housing in the 80% - 120% range of the median annual adjusted gross income, which properties would otherwise be eligible for an exemption from ad valorem taxation pursuant to Section 196.1978(3)(d)1.a., Florida Statutes. Resolution adopted by the City of Tampa, Florida, pursuant to Subsection 196.1978(3)(o), Florida Statutes, electing to &quot;Opt-Out&quot; of the property tax exemption for certain multifamily projects that provide affordable housing in the 80%-120% range of median annual adjusted gross income, which properties would otherwise be eligible for an exemption from ad valorem taxation pursuant to Subsection 196.1978(3)(D)1.a, Florida Statutes.</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>Section 196.1978(3), Florida Statutes, requires the Hillsborough County Property Appraiser to exempt certain qualifying rental properties from ad valorem taxation when such properties meet the statutory criteria for multifamily projects that provide affordable housing to households within specified income limits. Section 196.1978(3)(d)1.a. of the Florida Statutes provides a 75% ad valorem tax exemption es (i.e. 75% of the assessed property value is exempt from property taxes)  for affordable housing that serves persons or families whose annual household income is greater than 80 percent but not more than 120 percent of the median annual adjusted gross income (&quot;AMI&quot;)  for households within that metropolitan statistical area(&quot;MSA&quot;).</p>
<p>However, in its 2024 session, the Florida Legislature adopted a bill (HB 7073, Laws of Florida Ch. 2024-158)  that allows taxing authorities such as the City of Tampa to annually &quot;opt-out&quot; of providing that exemption if the three previous years Shimberg Center for Housing Studies Annual Reports which are prepared at the University of Florida per Sec. 420.6075, Florida Statutes (&quot;Shimberg Annual Reports&quot;) determine that the number of available afforable units is an MSA is greater than the number of renter households in the MSA for the category entitled 0-120% AMI.  It is necessary to hold a public hearing on the proposed resolution to receive public comment and allow the Tampa City Council to consider whether to approve the proposed opt-out of the 80–120 Tax Exemption.</p>
</details>

<article class="agenda-item" id="item-28424">
<h3 class="agenda-item__head"><a class="agenda-item__anchor" href="#item-28424">86</a> File No. F26-28424</h3>
<p>(Placed under Staff Reports and Unfinished Business pursuant to a motion made by Council.) Resolution approving two Program Signature Forms and Associated Contract Documents (including Enterprise Enrollment Form, Enterprise Amendment, Server and Cloud Enrollment and two Product Selection Forms) between the City of Tampa and Microsoft Corporation, allowing for the direct purchase of Microsoft Licenses and Products from Microsoft Corporation’s reseller, CDW Government LLC, through the State of Florida Alternate Contract No. 43230000-23-NASPO-ACS, and approving CDW Government LLC&#39;s Enterprise Quote for the Provision of Renewal of Microsoft Enterprise Subscription Licensing and Software Assurance in the estimated amount of $6,575,598.39 for use by the Technology and Innovation Department; authorizing the Mayor to execute said Program Signature Forms and associated documents (including Enterprise Enrollment Form, Enterprise Amendment, Server and Cloud Enrollment and two Product Selection Forms).</p>
<details class="agenda-item__details">
<summary>Item details</summary>
<section class="agenda-item-financial" aria-labelledby="financial-28424">
<h4 id="financial-28424" class="agenda-item-section__heading">Financial impact</h4>
<p class="agenda-item-financial__statement">That funding for the purchase of the renewal of Microsoft Enterprise Subscription Licensing and Software Assurance in the estimated amount of $2,191,866.13 in FY2027 for use by the Technology and Innovation Department shall be provided through the General Fund. Funding for the annual subscription licensing and assurance for years two through three in the estimated amount of $2,191,866.13 in FY2028 and $2,191,866.13 in FY2029, is subject to annual appropriations.</p>
<dl class="agenda-item-financial__totals"><div class="agenda-item-financial__row" title="Total dollars this resolution authorizes the City to spend in the current fiscal year. Inter-fund transfers are excluded to avoid double-counting."><dt>Spending authorized (FY27)</dt><dd>$2,191,866</dd></div></dl>
<p class="agenda-item-financial__future">Plus <strong>$4,383,732</strong> in future-year amounts subject to annual Council approval.</p>
<h5 class="agenda-item-financial__sources-heading">Funding sources</h5>
<ul class="agenda-item-financial__sources"><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-expenditure "><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$2,191,866.13</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">General Fund</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">Technology Infrastructure</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Principal Payment - Intangible Right to Use Subscription Asset</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">FY27 · 01100.242800.571007</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Estimated Expenditure</span></li><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-expenditure is-future"><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$2,191,866.13</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">General Fund</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">Technology Infrastructure</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Principal Payment - Intangible Right to Use Subscription Asset</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">FY28 · 01100.242800.571007</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Estimated Expenditure, subject to annual appropriation</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__subject" title="Council is not committing this fiscal year today; subject to future-year appropriation.">subject to annual appropriation</span></li><li class="agenda-item-financial__source is-expenditure is-future"><span class="agenda-item-financial__amount">$2,191,866.13</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__fund">General Fund</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__dept">Technology Infrastructure</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__object">Principal Payment - Intangible Right to Use Subscription Asset</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__meta">FY29 · 01100.242800.571007</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__marker">Estimated Expenditure, subject to annual appropriation</span><span class="agenda-item-financial__subject" title="Council is not committing this fiscal year today; subject to future-year appropriation.">subject to annual appropriation</span></li></ul>
</section>
<p class="agenda-item__label">Background</p>
<p>The Microsoft Enterprise Agreements (MS EA) are a vital three-year renewal subscription that is required for the City’s data center servers, Windows11 Enterprise 25H2 desktop operating systems, email, security authentication and desktop and cloud Office365 business software and networked file storage for all Microsoft office applications in use by the City. This renewal occurs in three-year intervals and is necessary to license these systems for ongoing use. If the subscription lapses, expires or otherwise is not renewed, access to the city applications, email and data will cease.</p>
<p>The City desires to renew the Microsoft Enterprise Enrollment for Software Assurance and Subscription Licenses allowing for the direct purchase of the Microsoft Licenses and Products from Microsoft’s reseller, CDW Government, LLC. through the State of Florida Contract #43230000-23-NASPO-ACS for Microsoft License, Maintenance, and Services.</p>
<p>Equal Business Opportunity (EBO) Statement: There was no EBO participation, as this purchase was made under the State of Florida Contract #43230000-23-NASPO-ACS.</p>
</details>
